2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThree qualities matter most for agent workloads, and Moli brings them together:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFull-featured\u003c/strong\u003e — real JavaScript, DOM, CSS, networking, storage, layout,\nscreenshots, and standard automation protocols, all integrated into one\nheadless browser.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFast\u003c/strong\u003e — most automation requests never need visual rendering, so\nstructure-first operations skip layout and paint entirely.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eResource-efficient\u003c/strong\u003e — layout and pixels are generated only when needed,\nso Moli does not have to continuously maintain and update a fully rendered\nvisual state.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat most browser automation tasks actually need is page structure, not a\ncontinuously rendered visual world. Moli treats the native DOM and style state\nas the single source of truth, triggering layout or software paint only for\noperations that genuinely require them.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eAgent request\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhat Moli does\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eExtract HTML/Markdown, query the DOM, run JS, inspect network/storage\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReads browser runtime state directly — does not trigger layout or paint\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRead an element's box, hit-test coordinates, send coordinate input\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRuns one layout calculation and retains only the latest frozen layout tree\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCapture a screenshot\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRebuilds from the current DOM/style, replaces the frozen tree, renders a fresh frame, and discards its paint state after use\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePoll a screencast\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCompares generation metadata only; clean state emits no frame, while changed state rebuilds and emits one fresh frame\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n  \u003ca href=\"/lexmount/moli/blob/main/assets/moli_ondemand_rendering_flow.svg\"\u003e\n    \u003cimg src=\"/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/moli_ondemand_rendering_flow.svg\" alt=\"How Moli handles a request: DOM-first by default, with layout and paint built fresh only on demand\" width=\"680\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\n  \u003c/a\u003e\n\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMoli still includes the complete set of capabilities: V8, CSS, layout, text\nshaping, hit-testing, software paint, and more. The only difference is \u003cem\u003ewhen\u003c/em\u003e\nvisual work runs and \u003cem\u003ehow long\u003c/em\u003e its results are retained. This cost model is\nespecially well suited to crawling, browser-use agents, retrieval pipelines,\nevaluation environments, and reinforcement-learning workloads.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCurrent capabilities\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-current-capabilities\" 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eComplete web runtime\u003c/strong\u003e — streaming HTML parsing, native DOM, V8 JavaScript,\nmodules/timers/microtasks/events, iframes and workers, CSS cascade,\nFetch/XHR/WebSocket, cookies, WebCrypto, and profile-scoped storage\n(localStorage, IndexedDB, OPFS).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExtraction-optimized outputs\u003c/strong\u003e — the CLI directly produces HTML, Markdown,\nJSON, semantic text trees, and frame-aware serialization, with\nselector/script/response waits and network tracing.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUnified automation binary\u003c/strong\u003e — CDP, WebDriver Classic, and WebDriver BiDi\nshare the same kernel and scheduler. No separate ChromeDriver, geckodriver,\nor browser installation is required.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReal visual capabilities on demand\u003c/strong\u003e — add \u003ccode\u003e--layout\u003c/code\u003e to enable complete box\nconstruction, Taffy layout, Parley text layout, layout-backed\nhit-testing/input, viewport\nscreenshots, and low-frequency CPU-rendered DevTools screencast frames.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eControllable operational options\u003c/strong\u003e — profiles, cookies, HTTP cache, proxies,\nresource families, connection limits, timeouts, private-network policy,\nuser-agent overrides, diagnostic logging, and network diagnostics are all\navailable.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv browser operations in Moli require an explicit opt-in and are never\nenabled by default:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eMode or option\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eBehavior\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDefault\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eLayoutPolicy::Mock\u003c/code\u003e screencast\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--resource\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFetch all optional visual/media resource families\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--image\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--font\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--audio\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--video\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--media\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--text-track\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnable one specific optional resource \u003ccode\u003e--cookie-file\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSelectively enable the persistence required by the workload\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLayout is an on-demand snapshot rather than continuously maintained state. The\nfirst geometry request (a cold start) builds a working layout tree from the\ncurrent DOM/style, freezes its canonical geometry into an immutable,\nDOM-independent \u003ccode\u003eFrozenLayoutTree\u003c/code\u003e, and retains only that latest tree. Ordinary\ngeometry reads may reuse it even if the page has changed. Screenshots always\nrebuild and replace the frozen tree. Each screencast subscription remembers\nonly an opaque visual-state token: an unchanged token suppresses the poll,\nwhile a changed token triggers one fresh frame. Paint results are never reused.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eArchitecture\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-architecture\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Architecture\" href=\"#architecture\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMoli is a standalone browser kernel, not a Chromium wrapper. It is built in\nRust, has its own ownership and lifecycle rules, and relies on:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003elibcurl\u003c/code\u003e — network transport and multi-request runtime\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehtml5ever\u003c/code\u003e — HTML parsing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003erusty_v8\u003c/code\u003e / V8 — JavaScript execution\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eServo/Stylo — selectors, cascade, computed style\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTaffy + Parley — box and text layout\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAnyRender/Vello CPU, \u003ccode\u003eusvg\u003c/code\u003e, and the Rust image ecosystem — software rendering\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDocument and style have a single source of truth: the native DOM and its Stylo\nintegration. Every real refresh builds a temporary working tree from that\nsource, optionally produces and consumes one fresh paint snapshot, freezes the\ncanonical box/fragment geometry into a compact \u003ccode\u003eFrozenLayoutTree\u003c/code\u003e, and discards\nthe working tree, style borrows, layout caches, diagnostics, and paint state.\nScreencast tokens contain generation metadata, never layout or paint data.\nSource lookup and hit-test candidates are derived from the frozen tree when\nqueried. The system has no incrementally maintained layout tree, damage graph,\nretained display list, GPU compositor, or persistent window.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBenchmark\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-benchmark\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Benchmark\" href=\"#benchmark\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe following measurements show Moli's current capability envelope. href=\"https://github.com/lexmount/Lexbench-Headless-Browser\"\u003eLexbench-Headless-Browser\u003c/a\u003e\ncorpus contains 1,928 tasks covering raw CDP, 13 pinned automation tools\nincluding Playwright, Puppeteer and Selenium, and web-platform semantics. To\ninclude Kitesurf, which is only available as a remote endpoint, the chart below\nuses 1,308 comparable tasks. All browsers use the same task selection rules.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cthemed-picture data-catalyst-inline=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpicture\u003e\n  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-five-engine-caliber-b-dark.jpg\"\u003e\n  \u003cimg alt=\"Task success rate of five headless browsers over 1,308 comparable tasks: Chrome 99.8%, Moli 81.9%, Kitesurf 62.1%, Lightpanda 53.3%, Obscura 44.9%\" src=\"/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-five-engine-caliber-b-light.jpg\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\n\u003c/picture\u003e\u003c/themed-picture\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMoli 0.1.1 passed 1,071 tasks, a success rate of 81.88%\u003c/strong\u003e, ahead of Kitesurf\nat 62.08%, Lightpanda at 53.29%, and Obscura at 44.88%; Chrome, the reference\nengine, reached 99.85%. Kitesurf ran at k=1, uncovered tasks count as not\npassed, and the reproduction conditions of a remote service differ from those\nof local binaries. See the benchmark's\n\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/lexmount/Lexbench-Headless-Browser/blob/kitesurf-eval/docs/reports/five-engine-report-20260813.md\"\u003efive-engine report\u003c/a\u003e\nfor the full results.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cthemed-picture data-catalyst-inline=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpicture\u003e\n  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-efficiency-map-dark.jpg\"\u003e\n  \u003cimg alt=\"Task success rate plotted against median peak memory per task for the four local engines: Chrome at 99.9% and 697 MiB, Moli at 80.7% and 92 MiB, Lightpanda at 43.8% and 34 MiB, Obscura at 39.5% and 39 MiB\" src=\"/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-efficiency-map-light.jpg\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\n\u003c/picture\u003e\u003c/themed-picture\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eKitesurf is a remote service, so its CPU, memory, and process counts cannot be\nmeasured. The resource comparison therefore covers only the four local\nengines. A separate 557-task run includes only work completed by all four.\nMoli's median CPU time per task was \u003cstrong\u003e100.6 ms\u003c/strong\u003e and its median peak memory was\n\u003cstrong\u003e92 MiB\u003c/strong\u003e; Chrome recorded \u003cstrong\u003e687 ms\u003c/strong\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003e697 MiB\u003c/strong\u003e, respectively. Moli used\nabout 15% of Chrome's CPU time and 13% of its peak memory. See the benchmark's\n\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/lexmount/Lexbench-Headless-Browser/blob/main/docs/reports/resource-card-20260812.md\"\u003eresource card\u003c/a\u003e\nfor the methodology and Moli is ready\nfor production use and remains under active development.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIts current intentional boundaries include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNo GUI browser, persistent window, GPU compositor, or retained multi-frame\npaint architecture.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt does not pursue pixel-for-pixel parity with Chrome or provide\nhigh-fidelity Canvas/WebGL/media playback.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--layout\u003c/code\u003e supports software screenshots and raster-backed CDP PDF\ngeneration, but not every Chrome screenshot or print mode is implemented.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUnsupported protocol paths return explicit errors — Moli never <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 align="center" tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Moli</h1><a id="user-content-moli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Moli" href="#moli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <strong>English</strong> |
  <a href="/lexmount/moli/blob/main/docs/README.zh-CN.md">简体中文</a> |
  <a href="/lexmount/moli/blob/main/docs/README.ja.md">日本語</a> |
  <a href="/lexmount/moli/blob/main/docs/README.de.md">Deutsch</a> |
  <a href="/lexmount/moli/blob/main/docs/README.fr.md">Français</a> |
  <a href="/lexmount/moli/blob/main/docs/README.es.md">Español</a>
</p>
<p dir="auto">Moli is a production-ready headless browser for AI agents. Its on-demand layout
and rendering design combines a complete browser runtime with a lightweight
resource footprint.</p>
<p dir="auto">Moli helps your AI agent fetch and extract web pages, search the web, and
automate browser tasks.</p>
<p dir="auto">Use it through the CLI, CDP, WebDriver Classic, or WebDriver BiDi.</p>
<p dir="auto">Moli supports Linux, macOS, and Windows.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Give this prompt to your AI agent:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Install the skills under https://github.com/lexmount/moli/tree/main/skills,
follow their instructions to download and install the latest prebuilt Moli
binary, then use moli-webfetch to fetch https://example.com and show me the
result."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Install the skills under https://github.com/lexmount/moli/tree/main/skills,
follow their instructions to download and install the latest prebuilt Moli
binary, then use moli-webfetch to fetch https://example.com and show me the
result.
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Direct installation</h3><a id="user-content-direct-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Direct installation" href="#direct-installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">On Linux or macOS:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -fsSL \
  https://github.com/lexmount/moli/releases/latest/download/moli-installer.sh | sh"><pre>curl --proto <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>=https<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> --tlsv1.2 -fsSL \
  https://github.com/lexmount/moli/releases/latest/download/moli-installer.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">On Windows, run in PowerShell:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="powershell -ExecutionPolicy ByPass -c &quot;irm https://github.com/lexmount/moli/releases/latest/download/moli-installer.ps1 | iex&quot;"><pre>powershell <span class="pl-k">-</span>ExecutionPolicy ByPass <span class="pl-k">-</span>c <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>irm https://github.com/lexmount/moli/releases/latest/download/moli-installer.ps1 | iex<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Showcase</h2><a id="user-content-showcase" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Showcase" href="#showcase"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI usage</h2><a id="user-content-cli-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI usage" href="#cli-usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Extract a page</h3><a id="user-content-extract-a-page" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Extract a page" href="#extract-a-page"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Render the page as Markdown with Moli's default completion strategy:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="moli fetch \
  --dump markdown \
  --wait-until done \
  https://example.com"><pre>moli fetch \
  --dump markdown \
  --wait-until <span class="pl-k">done</span> \
  https://example.com</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or directly return a compact, model-friendly semantic tree:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="moli fetch \
  --dump semantic_tree_text \
  --wait-selector body \
  https://example.com"><pre>moli fetch \
  --dump semantic_tree_text \
  --wait-selector body \
  https://example.com</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For visual output, enable on-demand layout and write a viewport PNG, a full-document PNG, or a paginated PDF:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="moli fetch --layout --dump screenshot https://example.com &gt; page.png
moli fetch --layout --dump screenshot_full https://example.com &gt; full-page.png
moli fetch --layout --dump pdf https://example.com &gt; page.pdf"><pre>moli fetch --layout --dump screenshot https://example.com <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> page.png
moli fetch --layout --dump screenshot_full https://example.com <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> full-page.png
moli fetch --layout --dump pdf https://example.com <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> page.pdf</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Run <code>fetch --help</code> for the complete option list, including output formats,
page-load/response waits, profiles, proxy settings, resource policies, and
tracing options.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Start the automation server</h3><a id="user-content-start-the-automation-server" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Start the automation server" href="#start-the-automation-server"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Basic automation server for DOM-first workloads
moli serve

# Enable real geometry, coordinate input, and screenshot/screencast surfaces
moli serve --layout

# Also fetch optional image, font, audio, video, media, and text-track resources
moli serve --layout --resource"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Basic automation server for DOM-first workloads</span>
moli serve

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Enable real geometry, coordinate input, and screenshot/screencast surfaces</span>
moli serve --layout

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Also fetch optional image, font, audio, video, media, and text-track resources</span>
moli serve --layout --resource</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The same endpoint serves all three protocols: CDP, WebDriver Classic, and
WebDriver BiDi. Playwright can connect directly over CDP:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import { chromium } from &quot;playwright&quot;;

const browser = await chromium.connectOverCDP(&quot;http://127.0.0.1:9222&quot;);
const context = browser.contexts()[0];
const page = context.pages()[0] ?? await context.newPage();

await page.goto(&quot;https://example.com&quot;);
console.log(await page.locator(&quot;body&quot;).innerText());

await browser.close();"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s1">chromium</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">"playwright"</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">chromium</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">connectOverCDP</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"http://127.0.0.1:9222"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">context</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">contexts</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">page</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">context</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">pages</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span> <span class="pl-c1">??</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">context</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newPage</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">page</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">goto</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"https://example.com"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">page</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">locator</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"body"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">innerText</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">close</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why Moli</h2><a id="user-content-why-moli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why Moli" href="#why-moli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Three qualities matter most for agent workloads, and Moli brings them together:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Full-featured</strong> — real JavaScript, DOM, CSS, networking, storage, layout,
screenshots, and standard automation protocols, all integrated into one
headless browser.</li>
<li><strong>Fast</strong> — most automation requests never need visual rendering, so
structure-first operations skip layout and paint entirely.</li>
<li><strong>Resource-efficient</strong> — layout and pixels are generated only when needed,
so Moli does not have to continuously maintain and update a fully rendered
visual state.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">What most browser automation tasks actually need is page structure, not a
continuously rendered visual world. Moli treats the native DOM and style state
as the single source of truth, triggering layout or software paint only for
operations that genuinely require them.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Agent request</th>
<th>What Moli does</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Extract HTML/Markdown, query the DOM, run JS, inspect network/storage</td>
<td>Reads browser runtime state directly — does not trigger layout or paint</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Read an element's box, hit-test coordinates, send coordinate input</td>
<td>Runs one layout calculation and retains only the latest frozen layout tree</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Capture a screenshot</td>
<td>Rebuilds from the current DOM/style, replaces the frozen tree, renders a fresh frame, and discards its paint state after use</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Poll a screencast</td>
<td>Compares generation metadata only; clean state emits no frame, while changed state rebuilds and emits one fresh frame</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a href="/lexmount/moli/blob/main/assets/moli_ondemand_rendering_flow.svg">
    <img src="/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/moli_ondemand_rendering_flow.svg" alt="How Moli handles a request: DOM-first by default, with layout and paint built fresh only on demand" width="680" style="max-width: 100%;">
  </a>
</p>
<p dir="auto">Moli still includes the complete set of capabilities: V8, CSS, layout, text
shaping, hit-testing, software paint, and more. The only difference is <em>when</em>
visual work runs and <em>how long</em> its results are retained. This cost model is
especially well suited to crawling, browser-use agents, retrieval pipelines,
evaluation environments, and reinforcement-learning workloads.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Current capabilities</h2><a id="user-content-current-capabilities" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Current capabilities" href="#current-capabilities"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Complete web runtime</strong> — streaming HTML parsing, native DOM, V8 JavaScript,
modules/timers/microtasks/events, iframes and workers, CSS cascade,
Fetch/XHR/WebSocket, cookies, WebCrypto, and profile-scoped storage
(localStorage, IndexedDB, OPFS).</li>
<li><strong>Extraction-optimized outputs</strong> — the CLI directly produces HTML, Markdown,
JSON, semantic text trees, and frame-aware serialization, with
selector/script/response waits and network tracing.</li>
<li><strong>Unified automation binary</strong> — CDP, WebDriver Classic, and WebDriver BiDi
share the same kernel and scheduler. No separate ChromeDriver, geckodriver,
or browser installation is required.</li>
<li><strong>Real visual capabilities on demand</strong> — add <code>--layout</code> to enable complete box
construction, Taffy layout, Parley text layout, layout-backed
hit-testing/input, viewport
screenshots, and low-frequency CPU-rendered DevTools screencast frames.</li>
<li><strong>Controllable operational options</strong> — profiles, cookies, HTTP cache, proxies,
resource families, connection limits, timeouts, private-network policy,
user-agent overrides, diagnostic logging, and network diagnostics are all
available.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Moli's relationship with Lexmount</h2><a id="user-content-molis-relationship-with-lexmount" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Moli's relationship with Lexmount" href="#molis-relationship-with-lexmount"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Moli is Lexmount's open-source headless browser; Lexmount Browser is the managed
cloud runtime and control plane built around it.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>The open-source headless browser is fully usable without Lexmount Browser.</strong></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Cost controls</h2><a id="user-content-cost-controls" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cost controls" href="#cost-controls"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Expensive browser operations in Moli require an explicit opt-in and are never
enabled by default:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Mode or option</th>
<th>Behavior</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Default</td>
<td><code>LayoutPolicy::Mock</code> — deterministic geometry in a compatible format, with no real layout or paint</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--layout</code></td>
<td><code>LayoutPolicy::OnDemand</code> — real layout, geometry, hit-testing, coordinate input, screenshots, screencast</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--resource</code></td>
<td>Fetch all optional visual/media resource families</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--image</code>, <code>--font</code>, <code>--audio</code>, <code>--video</code>, <code>--media</code>, <code>--text-track</code></td>
<td>Enable one specific optional resource family</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--profile-dir</code>, <code>--http-cache-dir</code>, <code>--cookie-file</code></td>
<td>Selectively enable the persistence required by the workload</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Layout is an on-demand snapshot rather than continuously maintained state. The
first geometry request (a cold start) builds a working layout tree from the
current DOM/style, freezes its canonical geometry into an immutable,
DOM-independent <code>FrozenLayoutTree</code>, and retains only that latest tree. Ordinary
geometry reads may reuse it even if the page has changed. Screenshots always
rebuild and replace the frozen tree. Each screencast subscription remembers
only an opaque visual-state token: an unchanged token suppresses the poll,
while a changed token triggers one fresh frame. Paint results are never reused.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture</h2><a id="user-content-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture" href="#architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Moli is a standalone browser kernel, not a Chromium wrapper. It is built in
Rust, has its own ownership and lifecycle rules, and relies on:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>libcurl</code> — network transport and multi-request runtime</li>
<li><code>html5ever</code> — HTML parsing</li>
<li><code>rusty_v8</code> / V8 — JavaScript execution</li>
<li>Servo/Stylo — selectors, cascade, computed style</li>
<li>Taffy + Parley — box and text layout</li>
<li>AnyRender/Vello CPU, <code>usvg</code>, and the Rust image ecosystem — software rendering</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Document and style have a single source of truth: the native DOM and its Stylo
integration. Every real refresh builds a temporary working tree from that
source, optionally produces and consumes one fresh paint snapshot, freezes the
canonical box/fragment geometry into a compact <code>FrozenLayoutTree</code>, and discards
the working tree, style borrows, layout caches, diagnostics, and paint state.
Screencast tokens contain generation metadata, never layout or paint data.
Source lookup and hit-test candidates are derived from the frozen tree when
queried. The system has no incrementally maintained layout tree, damage graph,
retained display list, GPU compositor, or persistent window.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Benchmark</h2><a id="user-content-benchmark" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Benchmark" href="#benchmark"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following measurements show Moli's current capability envelope. They cover
real websites, automation clients, Chromium/WPT behavior checks, and a large
nextest regression suite.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Mixed public-web crawl test</h3><a id="user-content-mixed-public-web-crawl-test" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Mixed public-web crawl test" href="#mixed-public-web-crawl-test"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">WPT tests</h3><a id="user-content-wpt-tests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WPT tests" href="#wpt-tests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In the current WPT selection used to validate Moli's agent-browser scope, one
full run passed <strong>1.612 million tests</strong>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Moli's performance in Lexbench-Headless-Browser</h3><a id="user-content-molis-performance-in-lexbench-headless-browser" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Moli's performance in Lexbench-Headless-Browser" href="#molis-performance-in-lexbench-headless-browser"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The full <a href="https://github.com/lexmount/Lexbench-Headless-Browser">Lexbench-Headless-Browser</a>
corpus contains 1,928 tasks covering raw CDP, 13 pinned automation tools
including Playwright, Puppeteer and Selenium, and web-platform semantics. To
include Kitesurf, which is only available as a remote endpoint, the chart below
uses 1,308 comparable tasks. All browsers use the same task selection rules.</p>
<themed-picture data-catalyst-inline="true"><picture>
  <source media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" srcset="/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-five-engine-caliber-b-dark.jpg">
  <img alt="Task success rate of five headless browsers over 1,308 comparable tasks: Chrome 99.8%, Moli 81.9%, Kitesurf 62.1%, Lightpanda 53.3%, Obscura 44.9%" src="/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-five-engine-caliber-b-light.jpg" width="100%">
</picture></themed-picture>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Moli 0.1.1 passed 1,071 tasks, a success rate of 81.88%</strong>, ahead of Kitesurf
at 62.08%, Lightpanda at 53.29%, and Obscura at 44.88%; Chrome, the reference
engine, reached 99.85%. Kitesurf ran at k=1, uncovered tasks count as not
passed, and the reproduction conditions of a remote service differ from those
of local binaries. See the benchmark's
<a href="https://github.com/lexmount/Lexbench-Headless-Browser/blob/kitesurf-eval/docs/reports/five-engine-report-20260813.md">five-engine report</a>
for the full results.</p>
<themed-picture data-catalyst-inline="true"><picture>
  <source media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" srcset="/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-efficiency-map-dark.jpg">
  <img alt="Task success rate plotted against median peak memory per task for the four local engines: Chrome at 99.9% and 697 MiB, Moli at 80.7% and 92 MiB, Lightpanda at 43.8% and 34 MiB, Obscura at 39.5% and 39 MiB" src="/lexmount/moli/raw/main/assets/lexbench-efficiency-map-light.jpg" width="100%">
</picture></themed-picture>
<p dir="auto">Kitesurf is a remote service, so its CPU, memory, and process counts cannot be
measured. The resource comparison therefore covers only the four local
engines. A separate 557-task run includes only work completed by all four.
Moli's median CPU time per task was <strong>100.6 ms</strong> and its median peak memory was
<strong>92 MiB</strong>; Chrome recorded <strong>687 ms</strong> and <strong>697 MiB</strong>, respectively. Moli used
about 15% of Chrome's CPU time and 13% of its peak memory. See the benchmark's
<a href="https://github.com/lexmount/Lexbench-Headless-Browser/blob/main/docs/reports/resource-card-20260812.md">resource card</a>
for the methodology and full data.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project scope</h2><a id="user-content-project-scope" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project scope" href="#project-scope"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Within the agent-browser scenarios defined in its documentation, Moli is ready
for production use and remains under active development.</p>
<p dir="auto">Its current intentional boundaries include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>No GUI browser, persistent window, GPU compositor, or retained multi-frame
paint architecture.</li>
<li>It does not pursue pixel-for-pixel parity with Chrome or provide
high-fidelity Canvas/WebGL/media playback.</li>
<li><code>--layout</code> supports software screenshots and raster-backed CDP PDF
generation, but not every Chrome screenshot or print mode is implemented.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Unsupported protocol paths return explicit errors — Moli never pretends that a
browser action, event, network observation, or visual result occurred.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Star history</h2><a id="user-content-star-history" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Star history" href="#star-history"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
